province_event = {
	id = 24001
	title = "EVTNAME24001"
	desc = "EVTDESC24001"
	trigger = {
		controlled_by_rebels = yes
		culture = aborigine
		NOT = {
			is_core = D39
		}
		owner = {
			NOT = {
				tag = D39
			}
			D39 = {
				exists = no
				NOT = {
					any_core = {
						always = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA24001"
		if = {
			limit = {
				controlled_by_rebels = yes
				culture = aborigine
				NOT = {
					is_core = D39
				}
				owner = {
					NOT = {
						tag = D39
					}
					D39 = {
						exists = no
						NOT = {
							any_core = {
								always = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add_core = D39
			owner = {
				D39 = {
					add_accepted_culture = aborigine
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
province_event = {
	id = 24002
	title = "EVTNAME24002"
	desc = "EVTDESC24002"
	trigger = {
		controlled_by_rebels = yes
		NOT = {
			is_core = D39
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			is_core = D39
		}
		any_pop = {
			has_pop_culture = aborigine
		}
		owner = {
			NOT = {
				tag = D39
			}
			D39 = {
				OR = {
					accepted_culture = aborigine
					primary_culture = aborigine
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA24002"
		add_core = D39
	}
}

	desc = "EVTDESC24120"
	trigger = {
		controlled_by_rebels = yes
		culture = baganda
		NOT = {
			is_core = UGA
		}
		owner = {
			NOT = {
				tag = UGA
			}
			UGA = {
				exists = no
				primary_culture = baganda
				NOT = {
					any_core = {
						always = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA24120"
		if = {
			limit = {
				controlled_by_rebels = yes
				culture = baganda
				NOT = {
					is_core = UGA
				}
				owner = {

						exists = no

	id = 24144
	title = "EVTNAME24144"
	desc = "EVTDESC24144"
	trigger = {
		controlled_by_rebels = yes
		culture = bedouin
		NOT = {
			is_core = D13
		}
		owner = {
			NOT = {
				tag = D13
			}
			D13 = {
				exists = no
				NOT = {
					any_core = {
						always = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 72
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA24144"
		if = {
			limit = {
				controlled_by_rebels = yes
				culture = bedouin
				NOT = {
					is_core = D13
				}
				owner = {
					NOT = {
						tag = D13
					}
					D13 = {
						exists = no
						NOT = {
							any_core = {
								always = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add_core = D13
			owner = {
				D13 = {
					add_accepted_culture = bedouin
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = 24364
	title = "EVTNAME24364"
	desc = "EVTDESC24364"
	trigger = {
		controlled_by_rebels = yes
		NOT = {
			is_core = QUE
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			is_core = QUE
		}
		any_pop = {
			has_pop_culture = french_canadian
		}
		owner = {
			NOT = {
				tag = QUE
			}
			QUE = {
				OR = {
					accepted_culture = french_canadian
					primary_culture = french_canadian
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA24364"
		add_core = QUE
	}
}

	id = 25037
	title = "EVTNAME25037"
	desc = "EVTDESC25037"
	trigger = {
		controlled_by_rebels = yes
		culture = teda
		NOT = {
			is_core = D35
		}
		owner = {
			NOT = {
				tag = D35
			}
			D35 = {
				exists = no
				NOT = {
					any_core = {
						always = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA25037"
		if = {
			limit = {
				controlled_by_rebels = yes
				culture = teda
				NOT = {
					is_core = D35
				}
				owner = {
					NOT = {
						tag = D35
					}
					D35 = {
						exists = no
						NOT = {
							any_core = {
								always = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			add_core = D35
			owner = {
				D35 = {
					add_accepted_culture = teda
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
